## PRE-FLIGHT (execute ALL in parallel before reading anything)

```bash

docker ps --filter "name=mega-brain" --format "{{.Names}}: {{.Status}}"
curl -s http://localhost:8001/api/v2/heartbeat  # Chroma API v2 health
ls docs/bok/ 2>/dev/null                    # BoK suites disponíveis
wc -l HANDOFF.md                            # total lines — drives the read-from-end offset
```

Read in parallel (HANDOFF.md uses **read-from-end strategy** — SSP-01 v6.5.0; arquivo monolítico newest-first em ~3170+ linhas, leitura completa estoura limite de 25k tokens):
- `HANDOFF.md` with `limit=71` → Task State header + FIRST ACTION FOR NEXT AGENT (sempre estável, contém summary da fase atual selada)
- `HANDOFF.md` with `offset=<total_lines - 400>` `limit=400` → últimas 1-2 Records + Pending Actions + GraphRAG State + Infrastructure + Key Files (suffix sections always at the tail)
- `CLAUDE.md` (architecture rules, data flow, key files — incluindo MCORCH Master Execution Protocol + Survival Laws link)
- `.claude/context/sprint-priorities.md` (sprint goal, 4Cs snapshot, top gaps)
- `/home/ubuntu/.claude/projects/-home-gcrUX-htdocs-constellation-orchestra/memory/MEMORY.md` (memory index)

> ⚠️ **Auditoria histórica de seals antigos:** usar `Read` com offset arbitrário em HANDOFF.md (fora do fluxo padrão de pickup). Ordering newest-first: seal mais antigo ≈ linha 2805, seal mais recente logo após linha 71. Cada `## <Phase> Record (YYYY-MM-DD)` marca um seal. Manter o Read em chunks ≤ 400 linhas para preservar budget de tokens.

Also check for loose files in scratch/:
```bash
ls scratch/ 2>/dev/null && echo "⚠️ scratch/ has files — consider moving to .claude/scripts/db/" || echo "scratch/ clean ✅"
```

### BoK Gate check (v3 — MCORCH Master Execution Protocol)

For each BoK suite in `docs/bok/<slug>/`, verify completeness:
```bash
for slug in $(ls docs/bok/ 2>/dev/null); do
  required=(00-index 01-mrd 02-brd 03-prd 04-frd 05-sdd 06-data-model 07-process-flow 08-quality-metrics)
  missing=()
  for doc in "${required[@]}"; do
    [ -f "docs/bok/$slug/$doc.md" ] || missing+=("$doc")
  done
  [ ${#missing[@]} -eq 0 ] && echo "✅ $slug — BoK complete" || echo "⚠️ $slug — missing: ${missing[*]}"
done
```

If the user mentions working on a **new module / complex feature / integration / architectural change**:
- Check whether `docs/bok/<expected-slug>/` exists with all 9 docs.
- If missing: surface in the **ALERTAS** section as a critical block per CLAUDE.md MCORCH Master Execution Protocol.
- Recommend `/bok-scribe <idea summary>` before any code is written.

## Rules

- **TypeScript errors → report immediately.** If there are TS errors, list them under ALERTAS. Do not start implementing new features until errors are resolved (unless the user explicitly asks to proceed despite errors).
- **Unhealthy containers → report with triage.** Suggest the fix command if known (e.g., `docker compose restart mcorch-vector-engine` for chroma).
- **Uncommitted changes → describe, do not commit.** Only describe what exists in the working tree; committing is the user's call.
- **Keep the brief tight.** No padding. No repetition of CLAUDE.md architecture. The user already knows the architecture — they need the delta: what changed, what's broken, what's next.
- **Pending Actions priority order:** CRITICAL (before first client) > NON-CRITICAL > Phase Zeta > Core v3 > Epsilon v9.
- **Knowledge Mesh numbers:** use `GraphRAG State` section from HANDOFF.md as the source. Do not invent counts.

> 6566	### Embedding Model
> 6567	`openai/text-embedding-3-small`, `dimensions: 768` (truncated output via OpenRouter).
> 6568	Schema: `vector(768)` — HNSW cosine index.
> 6569	
> 6570	### Embedding Pipeline (Autônomo — 2026-05-03)
> 6571	Três camadas eliminam intervenção manual:
> 6572	1. **INSERT trigger** — `trg_mcorch_embed_on_insert` (pg_net) dispara `embed-mcorch-node` assincronamente em todo INSERT onde `embedding IS NULL`. Nunca bloqueia o INSERT.
> 6573	2. **Post-commit hook** — `.git/hooks/post-commit` roda `node scripts/ingest-codebase.ts --quiet` em background após cada commit → re-ingesta AST.
> 6574	3. **MeshControlPanel** — UI bottom-left do MatrixPage: "Sync Embeddings" (`trigger-embeddings`) e "Refresh Mesh Stats" (`trigger-ingest`).
> 6575	
> 6576	### Comandos de manutenção da Mesh
> 6577	```bash
> 6578	# Verificar nós sem embedding (deveria retornar array vazio):
> 6579	curl -s "https://bcyvddsykvehvpwstlfa.supabase.co/rest/v1/mcorch_nodes?embedding=is.null&select=id,name" \
> 6580	  -H "apikey: $SB_SECRET_KEY" | python3 -c "import json,sys; d=json.load(sys.stdin); print(f'{len(d)} nós sem embedding')"
> 6581	
> 6582	# Backfill manual (Bun-native — requer .env):
> 6583	bun run scripts/backfill-embeddings.ts
> 6584	
> 6585	# Backfill de código-fonte nos nós AST:
> 6586	bun run scripts/backfill-node-content.ts
> 6587	
> 6588	# Re-sync AST completo:
> 6589	cd packages/gitnexus-core && npm run sync-mesh
> 6590	
> 6591	# Acompanhar re-ingesta pós-commit:
> 6592	tail -f /tmp/mcorch-ingest.log
> 6593	```

> 6597	## Video Rescue Protocol
> 6598	
> 6599	When a video generation operation (Google Veo) times out or client disconnects:
> 6600	```bash
> 6601	# Via edge function (production):
> 6602	curl -X POST "https://bcyvddsykvehvpwstlfa.supabase.co/functions/v1/rescue-video" \

> 6604	  -d '{"content_id":"<uuid>","operation_id":"<operation_name>"}'
> 6605	
> 6606	# Via local script (admin/debug):
> 6607	source .env && bun run rescue.ts <operation_id> <content_id>
> 6608	```

> 6700	## Constellation Matrix Intelligence & Stabilization Record (2026-05-03)
> 6701	
> 6702	Sessão de elevação da `ConstellationMatrix` ao nível de **Code Intelligence** e estabilização do motor gráfico. O motor Sigma.js/ForceAtlas2 apresentava loops de renderização infinitos ("blinking") e resets de estado ao interagir com a sidebar. A correção envolveu a estabilização de referências de props, memoização rigorosa das opções do grafo e desacoplamento de filtros dos ciclos de renderização do canvas. Paralelamente, a Matrix foi dotada de inteligência de código nativa via hook `useNodeIntelligence` e um `NodeDetailsPanel` reestruturado com navegação por abas.

> 32	  const admin = createClient(url, serviceKey);
> 33	  const logErr = () => admin.from("infra_health_logs").insert({ service: "intent-execute", status: "unhealthy" }).then(() => {}, () => {});
> 34	
> 35	  try {
> 36	    const authHeader = req.headers.get("Authorization");
> 37	    if (!authHeader) return json({ error: "Missing authorization" }, 401);
> 38	    const userClient = createClient(url, anonKey, { global: { headers: { Authorization: authHeader } } });
> 39	    const { data: { user }, error: authError } = await userClient.auth.getUser();
> 40	    if (authError || !user) return json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, 401);
> 41	
> 42	    const { plan_id, decision } = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));
> 43	    if (!plan_id || typeof plan_id !== "string") return json({ error: "plan_id é obrigatório" }, 422);
> 44	    if (!DECISIONS.includes(decision)) return json({ error: "decision deve ser approve, reject ou execute" }, 422);
> 45	
> 46	    // Tenant guard: load the plan scoped to the caller (service role + explicit user_id). Foreign plan → 404.

> 49	    if (loadErr) { logErr(); return json({ error: "Falha ao carregar o plano", detail: loadErr.message }, 500); }
> 50	    if (!row) return json({ error: "Plano não encontrado" }, 404);
> 51	
> 52	    const status: string = row.status;
> 53	
> 54	    // ── APPROVE: only a pending_hitl plan can be human-approved. ──
> 55	    if (decision === "approve") {
> 56	      if (status !== "pending_hitl") return json({ error: `Não é possível aprovar um plano em status "${status}"`, status }, 409);
> 57	      const { error } = await admin.from("intent_plans").update({ status: "approved" }).eq("id", plan_id).eq("user_id", user.id);
> 58	      if (error) { logErr(); return json({ error: "Falha ao aprovar", detail: error.message }, 500); }
> 59	      admin.from("infra_health_logs").insert({ service: "intent-execute", status: "healthy" }).then(() => {}, () => {});
> 60	      return json({ status: "ok", plan_id, plan_status: "approved" });
> 61	    }
> 62	
> 63	    // ── REJECT: terminal; allowed from any non-executed state (idempotent if already rejected). ──
> 64	    if (decision === "reject") {
> 65	      if (status === "executed") return json({ error: "Plano já executado não pode ser rejeitado", status }, 409);
> 66	      if (status === "rejected") return json({ status: "ok", plan_id, plan_status: "rejected", already: true });

> 70	      return json({ status: "ok", plan_id, plan_status: "rejected" });
> 71	    }
> 72	
> 73	    // ── EXECUTE: the security-critical path. ──
> 74	    if (status === "executed") return json({ status: "ok", plan_id, plan_status: "executed", already: true });
> 75	    if (status === "rejected") return json({ error: "Plano rejeitado não pode ser executado", status }, 409);
> 76	
> 77	    // DEFENSE-IN-DEPTH: re-run the SAME policy gate on the STORED plan. Never trust the persisted policy/status.
> 78	    // A high-impact plan executes ONLY if a human explicitly approved it (status='approved').
> 79	    const highImpact = planIsHighImpact(row.plan);
> 80	    if (highImpact && status !== "approved") {
> 81	      return json({
> 82	        error: "Plano de alto impacto exige aprovação humana (HITL) antes de executar.",
> 83	        code: "hitl_required", status,
> 84	      }, 409);
> 85	    }
> 86	
> 87	    const { error: updErr } = await admin.from("intent_plans")
> 88	      .update({ status: "executed" }).eq("id", plan_id).eq("user_id", user.id);
> 89	    if (updErr) { logErr(); return json({ error: "Falha ao marcar como executado", detail: updErr.message }, 500); }
> 90	
> 91	    // Knowledge Mesh observation (mandate: every orchestration run). Fire-and-forget.
> 92	    const taskCount = Array.isArray(row.plan) ? row.plan.length : 0;
> 93	    admin.from("mcorch_nodes").insert({
> 94	      node_type: "observation", name: `intent-exec-${plan_id}`,
> 95	      content: `Intent plan executed: "${String(row.intent).slice(0, 160)}" (${taskCount} tarefas · high_impact=${highImpact})`,
> 96	      user_id: user.id,
> 97	    }).then(() => {}, () => {});
> 98	    admin.from("infra_health_logs").insert({ service: "intent-execute", status: "healthy" }).then(() => {}, () => {});
> 99	
> 100	    // Governance done. Actual outward channel dispatch (campaign-run/orchestrate-content/nurture-dispatch) stays
> 101	    // GATED in this MVP — we return the validated manifest, we do NOT fabricate sends.

> **Tool Result (Read)** - ✅ Success
>
> ```
> 1	// Shared policy gate for the Intent Orchestrator (FR-MH-009). SSOT: docs/bok/marketing-hub/04-frd.md FR-MH-009 +
> 2	// 05-sdd.md STRIDE "ação de alto impacto sem revisão = Tampering → policy engine + HITL obrigatório".
> 3	//
> 4	// This is the SINGLE source of the high-impact decision. `intent-orchestrate` runs it to set policy/status at
> 5	// creation; `intent-execute` re-runs it at execution time as defense-in-depth (NEVER trust the stored
> 6	// policy/status — an outward action must clear this gate again before it can execute).
> 7	
> 8	export interface PlanTask {
> 9	  action: string;
> 10	  channel?: string;
> 11	  impact?: "low" | "medium" | "high";
> 12	  rationale?: string;
> 13	}
> 14	
> 15	// High-impact = outward / financial actions that MUST NOT run without human review (the HITL gate).
> 16	export const HIGH_IMPACT_RE =
> 17	  /(publi|send|e-?mail|post|tweet|spend|ad[_-]?spend|charge|cobr|envi|dispar|whats|launch|lança)/i;
> 18	
> 19	// Fail-safe channel rule: ANY outward channel (anything that is not the internal 'none'/'content') is high-impact,
> 20	// even if the AI mislabels impact=low or phrases the action verb to dodge the regex. Unknown channel → outward.
> 21	export const isOutwardChannel = (c?: string): boolean =>
> 22	  !!c && !["none", "content"].includes(c.toLowerCase());
> 23	
> 24	export function isHighImpactTask(t: PlanTask): boolean {
> 25	  return t?.impact === "high" || HIGH_IMPACT_RE.test(t?.action ?? "") || isOutwardChannel(t?.channel);
> 26	}
> 27	
> 28	/**
> 29	 * True if ANY task in the plan is high-impact → mandatory human approval (HITL) before execution.
> 30	 * FAIL-CLOSED: a malformed/non-array plan (e.g. a tampered jsonb object) is treated as high-impact, so the
> 31	 * re-validation in intent-execute can never be skipped by sending a plan shape the gate doesn't recognize.
> 32	 */
> 33	export function planIsHighImpact(tasks: unknown): boolean {
> 34	  if (!Array.isArray(tasks)) return true;
> 35	  return tasks.some((t) => isHighImpactTask(t as PlanTask));
> 36	}
> 37	
> ```

> 32	    const { data: { user }, error: authError } = await userClient.auth.getUser();
> 33	    if (authError || !user) return json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, 401);
> 34	
> 35	    const { intent } = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));
> 36	    if (!intent || typeof intent !== "string" || !intent.trim()) return json({ error: "intent é obrigatório" }, 422);
> 37	
> 38	    // resolve per-user AI key → system fallback (API Tenancy Model).
> 39	    const { data: keys } = await admin.from("decrypted_user_api_keys").select("*").eq("user_id", user.id).maybeSingle();
> 40	    const groqKey = keys?.groq_api_key || Deno.env.get("GROQ_API_KEY");
> 41	    const openRouterKey = keys?.openrouter_api_key || Deno.env.get("OPENROUTER_API_KEY");
> 42	    const geminiKey = keys?.google_api_key || Deno.env.get("GEMINI_API_KEY");
> 43	    const aiKey = groqKey || openRouterKey;
> 44	
> 45	    // generate the plan via AI (fail-soft to a single review task).
> 46	    let tasks: PlanTask[] = [];
> 47	    if (aiKey) {
> 48	      const isGroq = !!groqKey;
> 49	      try {
> 50	        const resp = await fetchLLMWithFallback(
> 51	          isGroq ? "https://api.groq.com/openai/v1/chat/completions" : "https://openrouter.ai/api/v1/chat/completions",
> 52	          {
> 53	            method: "POST",
> 54	            headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${aiKey}`, "Content-Type": "application/json", "HTTP-Referer": "https://mcorch.com", "X-Title": "GCRUX Constellation" },
> 55	            body: JSON.stringify({
> 56	              model: isGroq ? "llama-3.3-70b-versatile" : "meta-llama/llama-3.3-70b-instruct",
> 57	              messages: [
> 58	                { role: "system", content: "Você é um orquestrador de marketing. Dada uma intenção, gere um plano de 2 a 5 tarefas. Responda APENAS com JSON: {\"tasks\":[{\"action\":\"verbo curto\",\"channel\":\"email|social|content|affiliate|none\",\"impact\":\"low|medium|high\",\"rationale\":\"por que esta tarefa\"}]}. impact=high para publicar, enviar, cobrar ou veicular anúncio." },
> 59	                { role: "user", content: String(intent).slice(0, 800) },
> 60	              ],
> 61	              stream: false, temperature: 0.4,
> 62	            }),
> 63	          },
> 64	          { openRouterKey, geminiKey, groqKey, functionName: "intent-orchestrate" },
> 65	        );
> 66	        if (resp.ok) {
> 67	          const data = await resp.json();
> 68	          const raw: string = data?.choices?.[0]?.message?.content ?? "";
> 69	          const match = raw.match(/\{[\s\S]*\}/);
> 70	          if (match) {
> 71	            const parsed = JSON.parse(match[0]);
> 72	            if (Array.isArray(parsed?.tasks)) {
> 73	              tasks = parsed.tasks.slice(0, 5).map((t: PlanTask) => ({
> 74	                action: String(t.action ?? "task").slice(0, 120),
> 75	                channel: t.channel ? String(t.channel).slice(0, 40) : undefined,
> 76	                impact: (["low", "medium", "high"].includes(String(t.impact)) ? t.impact : "medium") as PlanTask["impact"],
> 77	                rationale: t.rationale ? String(t.rationale).slice(0, 300) : undefined,
> 78	              }));
> 79	            }
> 80	          }
> 81	        }
> 82	      } catch (_) { /* fail-soft below */ }
> 83	    }
> 84	    let heuristic = false;
> 85	    if (tasks.length === 0) {
> 86	      heuristic = true;
> 87	      tasks = [{ action: "revisar_intencao", channel: "none", impact: "low", rationale: "Plano heurístico (IA indisponível ou resposta inválida) — revisar a intenção manualmente." }];
> 88	    }
> 89	
> 90	    // ── POLICY ENGINE: any high-impact task → HITL (mandatory human approval; STRIDE Tampering mitigation) ──
> 91	    // Shared gate (_shared/intent-policy.ts) — intent-execute re-runs the SAME gate at execution time.
> 92	    const highImpact = planIsHighImpact(tasks);
> 93	    const policy: "auto" | "hitl" = highImpact ? "hitl" : "auto";
> 94	    const status = highImpact ? "pending_hitl" : "auto_approved";
> 95	    const rationale = heuristic
> 96	      ? "Gerado por heurística (sem IA) — requer revisão humana."
> 97	      : highImpact
> 98	        ? "O plano contém ao menos uma tarefa de alto impacto (publicar/enviar/cobrar/anúncio) → aprovação humana obrigatória (HITL) antes de executar."
> 99	        : "Apenas tarefas de baixo/médio impacto → auto-aprovado para execução.";
> 100	
> 101	    // persist (RLS-scoped via service role + explicit user_id).
> 102	    const { data: row, error: insErr } = await admin
> 103	      .from("intent_plans")
> 104	      .insert({ user_id: user.id, intent: intent.slice(0, 2000), plan: tasks, policy, status, rationale })
> 105	      .select("id").single();
> 106	    if (insErr || !row) {
> 107	      admin.from("infra_health_logs").insert({ service: "intent-orchestrate", status: "unhealthy" }).then(() => {}, () => {});
> 108	      return json({ error: "Falha ao persistir o plano", detail: insErr?.message }, 500);
> 109	    }
> 110	
> 111	    // observation node into the Knowledge Mesh (mandate: every orchestration run). Fire-and-forget.
> 112	    admin.from("mcorch_nodes").insert({
> 113	      node_type: "observation", name: `intent-plan-${row.id}`,
> 114	      content: `Intent: ${intent.slice(0, 200)} → policy=${policy} status=${status} (${tasks.length} tarefas)`,
> 115	      user_id: user.id,
> 116	    }).then(() => {}, () => {});
> 117	    admin.from("infra_health_logs").insert({ service: "intent-orchestrate", status: "healthy" }).then(() => {}, () => {});
> 118	
> 119	    return json({ status: "ok", plan_id: row.id, policy, plan_status: status, tasks, rationale, source: heuristic ? "heuristic" : "ai" });
> 120	  } catch (err) {
> 121	    admin.from("infra_health_logs").insert({ service: "intent-orchestrate", status: "unhealthy" }).then(() => {}, () => {});
> 122	    return json({ error: "Erro interno no intent-orchestrate", detail: String((err as Error)?.message ?? err) }, 500);
> 123	  }
> 124	});
> 125	
> ```

>
> ```
> 1	# SOP — Intent Plan Execution (HITL approve / reject / execute)
> 2	
> 3	> **Lei 2 (Processo Antecipado).** Documenta o processo humano da metade HITL do FR-MH-009 (Intent Orchestrator) **antes** do `intent-execute`. SSOT: `docs/bok/marketing-hub/04-frd.md` FR-MH-009 + `05-sdd.md` (§intent-execute + STRIDE "ação de alto impacto sem revisão = Tampering → policy engine + HITL obrigatório") + `06-data-model.md` (§intent_plans status machine).
> 4	
> 5	## Contexto
> 6	
> 7	`intent-orchestrate` (FR-MH-009) traduz uma intenção em linguagem natural num **plano de tarefas** e roda um **policy engine**: qualquer tarefa de **alto impacto** (publicar / enviar / cobrar / veicular anúncio / canal outward) força `policy='hitl'` + `status='pending_hitl'`; senão `policy='auto'` + `status='auto_approved'`. O plano fica persistido em `intent_plans` com um `rationale` (XAI auditável). Este SOP cobre o que acontece **depois**: aprovar, rejeitar ou executar o plano.
> 8	
> 9	Máquina de estados (`intent_plans.status`):
> 10	
> 11	```
> 12	proposed ──▶ auto_approved ─────────────────────────▶ executed
> 13	        └──▶ pending_hitl ──▶ approved ─────────────▶ executed
> 14	                          └──▶ rejected  (terminal)
> 15	```
> 16	
> 17	## Operator
> 18	
> 19	Quem executa hoje: o **tenant** (Usuário Zero / operador de marketing) revisando os planos no **Marketing Hub** (`/dashboard/marketing`, painel Intent Orchestrator). A decisão HITL é **humana e consciente** — é a salvaguarda contra uma ação outward disparar sem revisão.
> 20	
> 21	## Sequence (cada passo com critério material de sucesso)
> 22	
> 23	| # | Passo | Ação | Critério de sucesso material |
> 24	|---|-------|------|------------------------------|
> 25	| 1 | **Orquestrar** | Operator descreve a intenção → `intent-orchestrate` | HTTP 200 `{plan_id, policy, plan_status, tasks, rationale}` + linha em `intent_plans` |
> 26	| 2 | **Triagem auto** | Se `policy='auto'` (só tarefas low/medium, nenhum canal outward) | `status='auto_approved'` — não exige humano |
> 27	| 3 | **Triagem HITL** | Se `policy='hitl'` (≥1 tarefa de alto impacto) | `status='pending_hitl'` — aparece no painel HITL pendente |
> 28	| 4 | **Decidir** | Operator clica **Aprovar** ou **Rejeitar** → `intent-execute {decision}` | `status='approved'` ou `'rejected'` (transição validada server-side) |
> 29	| 5 | **Executar** | Operator clica **Executar** → `intent-execute {decision:'execute'}` | `intent-execute` **re-valida** o outward; se passa, `status='executed'` |
> 30	
> 31	## Verification gates (defesa-em-profundidade — a regra de ouro)
> 32	
> 33	1. **Transições válidas server-side.** `intent-execute` recusa transições inválidas (`approve` só de `pending_hitl`; `execute` nunca de `rejected`; `executed` é idempotente). Retorna `409` com mensagem PT-BR.
> 34	2. **Re-validação no execute (NÃO confiar em `auto_approved`).** O `execute` **recomputa** o high-impact a partir do `plan` armazenado (mesmo gate do `intent-orchestrate`, via `_shared/intent-policy.ts`). Se o plano contém ação de alto impacto **e** `status !== 'approved'`, recusa com `409 {code:'hitl_required'}` — mesmo que a linha diga `policy='auto'`/`status='auto_approved'` (proteção contra mislabel da IA **ou** adulteração direta do registro via PostgREST RLS).
> 35	3. **Tenant guard.** `intent-execute` carrega o plano por `id` **E** `user_id = auth.uid()` (service role + escopo explícito). Plano de outro tenant → `404`.
> 36	4. **Outward dispatch é gated.** Nesta MVP o `execute` faz a **governança** (re-validação + transição + observação no mesh); o disparo real de canal (`campaign-run`/`orchestrate-content`/`nurture-dispatch`) permanece **gated** (igual à entrega externa de nurturing) — o executor devolve o manifesto das tarefas validadas, sem fabricar envio.
> 37	
> 38	## Recovery path (falha no passo N)
> 39	
> 40	- **Transição inválida (409):** o estado não muda; o Operator corrige (ex.: aprovar antes de executar). Nada a fazer no banco.
> 41	- **Re-validação bloqueia (409 `hitl_required`):** o plano permanece `pending_hitl`/`auto_approved`; o Operator deve **Aprovar** explicitamente antes de re-executar. Esse bloqueio é o comportamento correto, não um bug.
> 42	- **Falha de persistência (500):** `infra_health_logs.service='intent-execute' status='unhealthy'`; o status não avança; retry idempotente seguro (a transição é checada de novo).
> 43	
> 44	## Success signal (materialmente observável)
> 45	
> 46	`intent_plans.status='executed'` para o `plan_id` + um nó `observation` no Knowledge Mesh (`intent-exec-<plan_id>`) + `infra_health_logs.service='intent-execute' status='healthy'`. Um plano de alto impacto **só** chega a `executed` se passou por `approved` (humano) — verificável no `created_at`/`updated_at` e no histórico de status.
> 47	
> 48	## ORO
> 49	
> 50	- **Operator:** tenant (humano) no painel HITL · **Reviewer:** o próprio policy engine + re-validação server-side · **Owner:** Sovereign (blast radius = ação outward disparada sem revisão; mitigado pelo gate de re-validação).
> 51	
> ```

## 🤖 Claude *(01:49:04)*

Now I have the full picture of the intent layer. Critical finding for Step 1: the migration's RLS policy `intent_plans_update_own` lets the **authenticated owner UPDATE their own row including `status`** — so a tampering owner could `PATCH intent_plans?id=eq.X {status:'approved'}` via direct PostgREST, self-approving past the HITL gate, then the re-validation in `intent-execute` sees `status==='approved'` and passes. That's exactly the hole OTD-INTENT-TRANSITION-GUARD must close.

Before designing the dispatcher (Step 2), I must read the three real target functions' input contracts + verify BoK alignment + run the anti-drift mesh audit. Reading materially, not blind.

> 13	import { serve } from "https://deno.land/std@0.168.0/http/server.ts";
> 14	import { createClient } from "https://esm.sh/@supabase/supabase-js@2";
> 15	
> 16	const corsHeaders = {
> 17	  "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*",
> 18	  "Access-Control-Allow-Methods": "POST, OPTIONS",
> 19	  "Access-Control-Allow-Headers": "authorization, x-client-info, apikey, content-type",
> 20	};
> 21	
> 22	const CAMPAIGN_RUN_COST = 10; // mirrors COIN_COSTS.CAMPAIGN_RUN — the orchestration fee (Deno can't import src/lib).
> 23	const STEP_TIMEOUT_MS = 120_000; // per orchestrate-content dispatch (it generates content + image + publishes).
> 24	const MAX_PARALLEL_STEPS = 8;    // safety bound on simultaneous heavy dispatches; extras left idle (reported in mesh).
> 25	
> 26	interface StepRow {
> 27	  id: string;
> 28	  position: number;
> 29	  channel: string;
> 30	  action: string;
> 31	  status: string;
> 32	}
> 33	
> 34	// channel -> orchestrate-content platform names. 'email' is nurture-dispatch territory (SDD §3), not servable here.
> 35	function channelToPlatforms(channel: string): string[] {
> 36	  switch (channel) {
> 37	    case "content": return ["wordpress"];
> 38	    case "affiliate": return ["wordpress"]; // affiliate links attach to the WP article (orchestrate-content step 5).
> 39	    case "social": return ["linkedin", "twitter"];
> 40	    default: return []; // email / unknown -> not dispatchable via orchestrate-content.
> 41	  }
> 42	}
> 43	
> 44	serve(async (req) => {
> 45	  if (req.method === "OPTIONS") return new Response("ok", { headers: corsHeaders });
> 46	  const json = (body: unknown, status = 200) =>
> 47	    new Response(JSON.stringify(body), { status, headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" } });
> 48	
> 49	  const url = Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL")!;
> 50	  const serviceKey = (Deno.env.get("SB_SECRET_KEY") ?? Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY"))!;
> 51	  const anonKey = (Deno.env.get("SB_PUBLISHABLE_KEY") ?? Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_ANON_KEY"))!;
> 52	  const admin = createClient(url, serviceKey);
> 53	
> 54	  try {
> 55	    const authHeader = req.headers.get("Authorization");
> 56	    if (!authHeader) return json({ error: "Missing authorization" }, 401);
> 57	
> 58	    const userClient = createClient(url, anonKey, { global: { headers: { Authorization: authHeader } } });
> 59	    const { data: { user }, error: authError } = await userClient.auth.getUser();
> 60	    if (authError || !user) return json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, 401);
> 61	
> 62	    const { campaign_id } = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));
> 63	    if (!campaign_id || typeof campaign_id !== "string") return json({ error: "campaign_id é obrigatório" }, 422);
> 64	
> 65	    // existence + ownership (tenant-scoped); 404 also covers another tenant's campaign.
> 66	    const { data: campaign } = await admin
> 67	      .from("campaigns")
> 68	      .select("id, user_id, name, intent, status, mesh_node_id")
> 69	      .eq("id", campaign_id).eq("user_id", user.id).maybeSingle();
> 70	    if (!campaign) return json({ error: "Campanha não encontrada" }, 404);
> 71	
> 72	    // ordered steps (ascending position) — a campaign with no steps is a validation failure.
> 73	    const { data: steps } = await admin
> 74	      .from("campaign_steps")
> 75	      .select("id, position, channel, action, status")
> 76	      .eq("campaign_id", campaign_id).eq("user_id", user.id)
> 77	      .order("position", { ascending: true });
> 78	    if (!steps || steps.length === 0) return json({ error: "Campanha não tem passos para executar" }, 422);
> 79	
> 80	    // mcoCoins gatekeeper: the orchestration FEE (10) only — each dispatched step self-bills its own 10 (cascade).
> 81	    const { data: bal } = await admin.from("profiles").select("mco_balance").eq("id", user.id).maybeSingle();
> 82	    if ((bal?.mco_balance ?? 0) < CAMPAIGN_RUN_COST) return json({ error: "Saldo insuficiente de mcoCoins" }, 402);
> 83	    const { error: deductErr } = await admin.rpc("deduct_mco_coins", { p_user_id: user.id, p_amount: CAMPAIGN_RUN_COST });
> 84	    if (deductErr) return json({ error: "Saldo insuficiente de mcoCoins" }, 402);
> 85	
> 86	    const run_id = crypto.randomUUID();
> 87	    const topic = campaign.intent?.trim() || campaign.name; // orchestrate-content requires a topic; name is NOT NULL.
> 88	
> 89	    // running a draft makes the campaign active (best-effort; not in the response contract).
> 90	    await admin.from("campaigns").update({ status: "active" }).eq("id", campaign_id).eq("user_id", user.id);
> 91	
> 92	    const rows = steps as StepRow[];
> 93	    // Non-servable channels (email/unknown): block up front — never dispatched, never charged.
> 94	    const blocked = rows.filter((s) => channelToPlatforms(s.channel).length === 0);
> 95	    for (const s of blocked) {
> 96	      await admin.from("campaign_steps").update({ status: "blocked" }).eq("id", s.id).eq("user_id", user.id);
> 97	    }
> 98	    const dispatchable = rows.filter((s) => channelToPlatforms(s.channel).length > 0).slice(0, MAX_PARALLEL_STEPS);
> 99	    const cappedOut = rows.filter((s) => channelToPlatforms(s.channel).length > 0).length - dispatchable.length;
> 100	
> 101	    const orchestrateUrl = `${url}/functions/v1/orchestrate-content`;
> 102	    const results = await Promise.allSettled(
> 103	      dispatchable.map(async (step) => {
> 104	        await admin.from("campaign_steps").update({ status: "running" }).eq("id", step.id).eq("user_id", user.id);
> 105	        const platforms = channelToPlatforms(step.channel);
> 106	        const ctrl = new AbortController();
> 107	        const to = setTimeout(() => ctrl.abort(), STEP_TIMEOUT_MS);
> 108	        try {
> 109	          const resp = await fetch(orchestrateUrl, {
> 110	            method: "POST",
> 111	            headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", Authorization: authHeader },
> 112	            body: JSON.stringify({ topic, campaign_id, platforms, wp_status: "draft" }),
> 113	            signal: ctrl.signal,
> 114	          });
> 115	          const ok = resp.ok;
> 116	          await admin.from("campaign_steps").update({ status: ok ? "done" : "error" }).eq("id", step.id).eq("user_id", user.id);
> 117	          return ok;
> 118	        } catch (_) {
> 119	          await admin.from("campaign_steps").update({ status: "error" }).eq("id", step.id).eq("user_id", user.id);
> 120	          return false;
> 121	        } finally {
> 122	          clearTimeout(to);
> 123	        }
> 124	      }),
> 125	    );
> 126	    const stepsDispatched = results.filter((r) => r.status === "fulfilled" && r.value === true).length;
> 127	    const degraded = stepsDispatched < dispatchable.length || cappedOut > 0;
> 128	
> 129	    // Knowledge mesh mandate: every orchestration run inserts an observation node. Fail-soft — must not break the run.
> 130	    try {
> 131	      const { data: obsNode } = await admin.from("mcorch_nodes").insert({
> 132	        name: `campaign-run:${campaign_id}:${run_id}`,
> 133	        node_type: "observation",
> 134	        content:
> 135	          `Campaign "${campaign.name}" run ${run_id}: ${stepsDispatched}/${dispatchable.length} steps dispatched to ` +
> 136	          `orchestrate-content${blocked.length ? `, ${blocked.length} blocked (non-servable channel)` : ""}` +
> 137	          `${cappedOut > 0 ? `, ${cappedOut} left idle (parallel cap)` : ""}.`,
> 138	        stability_score: 1.0,
> 139	        metadata: {
> 140	          event: "campaign_run", campaign_id, run_id,
> 141	          steps_total: rows.length, steps_dispatched: stepsDispatched,
> 142	          steps_blocked: blocked.length, steps_capped: cappedOut,
> 143	        },
> 144	        user_id: user.id,
> 145	      }).select("id").single();
> 146	      if (obsNode && campaign.mesh_node_id) {
> 147	        await admin.from("mcorch_edges").insert({
> 148	          source_id: obsNode.id, target_id: campaign.mesh_node_id, relation_type: "observes", weight: 1.0,
> 149	        });
> 150	      }
> 151	    } catch (_) { /* fail-soft */ }
> 152	
> 153	    admin.from("infra_health_logs").insert({ service: "campaign-run", status: degraded ? "degraded" : "healthy" })
> 154	      .then(() => {}, () => {});
> 155	
> 156	    const { data: newBal } = await admin.from("profiles").select("mco_balance").eq("id", user.id).maybeSingle();
> 157	    return json({ status: "ok", campaign_id, run_id, steps_dispatched: stepsDispatched, new_balance: newBal?.mco_balance ?? null });
> 158	  } catch (err) {
> 159	    admin.from("infra_health_logs").insert({ service: "campaign-run", status: "unhealthy" }).then(() => {}, () => {});
> 160	    return json({ error: "Erro interno ao executar campanha", detail: String((err as Error)?.message ?? err) }, 500);
> 161	  }
> 162	});
> 163	
> ```

> 46	
> 47	    // pre-flight: check AI keys before charging user

> 49	    const groqKey = userKeys?.groq_api_key || Deno.env.get("GROQ_API_KEY");

> 70	      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: "topic is required" }), {

> 72	      });
> 73	    }

> 75	    // mcoCoins gatekeeper

> 78	      .from("profiles")

> 80	      .eq("id", user.id)
> 81	      .maybeSingle();
> 82

> 84	      return new Response(

> 86	        { status: 402, headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" } },
> 87	      );
> 88	    }
> 89	
> 90	    const { error: deductErr } = await admin.rpc("deduct_mco_coins", {
> 91	      p_user_id: user.id,
> 92	      p_amount: ORCHESTRATION_COST,
> 93	    });
> 94	    if (deductErr) {
> 95	      return new Response(
> 96	        JSON.stringify({ error: "Saldo insuficiente de mcoCoins para executar a orquestração" }),
> 97	        { status: 402, headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" } },
> 98	      );
> 99	    }
> 100	
> 101	    // utm_campaign uses the campaign_id for per-campaign attribution; fallback to slug for unnamed runs
> 102	    const utmCampaign = campaign_id
> 103	      ? encodeURIComponent(campaign_id)
> 104	      : `mcorch_${topic.slice(0, 24).replace(/\s+/g, "_").toLowerCase()}`;
> 105

> 109	      .from("pipeline_runs")

> 112	        topic,
> 113	        platforms,

> 128	      })
> 129	      .select("id")

> 134	    if (!runId) {

> 138	    // Determine first step in queue

> 140	    let firstStep: string | null = null;
> 141	    for (const candidate of stepsOrder) {

> 144	      } else if (candidate === "linkedin_post") {

> 148	      } else if (candidate === "knowledge_mesh") {
> 149	        firstStep = candidate; // Final fallback always runs
> 150	        break;
> 151	      }
> 152	    }
> 153

> 156	      await admin.rpc("async_orchestrate_step", {

> 181	    return new Response(

> 183	      { status: 500, headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" } }
> 184	    );

> 1	import type { SupabaseClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js';
> 2	
> 3	// mcoCoins cost per operation — single source of truth (frontend).
> 4	//
> 5	// CALIBRATION MODEL (2026-06-03 · OTD-MCOIN-CALIBRATION CLOSED) — derived from REAL provider cost, not round figures.
> 6	// SOP: docs/processes/[[mcoin-cost-calibration|mcoin-cost-calibration]].md.
> 7	//   formula:    mco(op) = ceil( real_cost_usd(op) / USD_PER_MCO_FLOOR × MARGIN )
> 8	//   floor rate: USD_PER_MCO_FLOOR = $0.018/mco — the CHEAPEST sale: Enterprise R$997 / 10000 mco ÷ R$5.5/USD.
> 9	//               (Starter R$147/500 = $0.053/mco and Pro R$397/2000 = $0.036/mco sell DEARER → more margin there.)
> 10	//   margin:     MARGIN = 4× (target gross markup at the worst-case sale price).
> 11	//   FX:         R$5.5/USD assumed; conclusions hold across R$5–7 except IMAGE near R$7 (kept at 4× headroom).
> 12	//
> 13	//   Measured real costs (USD): text llama-3.3-70b ≈ $0.001 (Groq ~free) · DALL·E 3 1024² image = $0.04 ·
> 14	//   orchestrate run (3 texts + 1 image) ≈ $0.04 · Higgsfield DoP 5s video = $0.56 (canvas-execute, validated 4×).
> 15	//   Per-op markup at the floor: ORCHESTRATION_RUN 10 → 4.4× ✓ · canvas video 125 → 4.0× ✓ · canvas image 12 → 5.4× ✓.
> 16	//   ORCHESTRATION_RUN stays a FLAT 10 (bundle): covers 3×CONTENT_GENERATION + IMAGE_GENERATION (sum 15) at a
> 17	//   deliberate bundle discount. Edge fns hardcode their own copies (Deno can't import src/lib) — keep in sync.
> 18	export const COIN_COSTS = {
> 19	  ORCHESTRATION_RUN: 10,   // 3 texts + 1 image, charged flat (bundle discount vs the 15 below). ~4.4× at floor.
> 20	  CONTENT_GENERATION: 2,   // 1 LLM article (~$0.0006). Strict floor is 1; held at 2 as a conservative minimum. (was 5)
> 21	  IMAGE_GENERATION: 9,     // 1 DALL·E 3 1024² image ($0.04 → 4× at floor). WAS 3 = 1.36× (under-margined) → fixed.
> 22	  EMBED_NODE: 1,           // 1 embedding call (~$0.000002). Floor minimum.
> 23	  LEAD_SCORE: 1,           // 1 LLM scoring call (~$0.0005). marketing-hub FR-MH-003 (lead-score edge fn mirrors this).
> 24	  CAMPAIGN_RUN: 10,        // marketing-hub FR-MH-004 — orchestration FEE on top of the cascaded per-step ORCHESTRATION_RUN.
> 25	  NURTURE_DISPATCH: 2,     // marketing-hub FR-MH-008 — 1 LLM message (~$0.0006). nurture-dispatch edge fn mirrors this.
> 26	} as const;
> 27	
> 28	export type CoinOperation = keyof typeof COIN_COSTS;
> 29	
> 30	/**
> 31	 * Deducts mcoCoins from the authenticated user's profile.
> 32	 * Calls the `deduct_mco_coins` SECURITY DEFINER RPC which enforces
> 33	 * the balance check atomically in the database.
> 34	 * Throws if balance is insufficient or the RPC fails.
> 35	 */
> 36	export async function deductMcoCoins(
> 37	  supabase: SupabaseClient,
> 38	  userId: string,
> 39	  amount: number,
> 40	): Promise<void> {
> 41	  const { error } = await supabase.rpc('deduct_mco_coins', {
> 42	    p_user_id: userId,
> 43	    p_amount: amount,
> 44	  });
> 45	
> 46	  if (error) {
> 47	    throw new Error(error.message || 'Falha ao deduzir mcoCoins');
> 48	  }
> 49	}
> 50	
> 51	/**
> 52	 * Returns the current mco_balance for the given user.
> 53	 * Returns 0 if the profile row is not found.
> 54	 */
> 55	export async function getMcoBalance(
> 56	  supabase: SupabaseClient,
> 57	  userId: string,
> 58	): Promise<number> {
> 59	  const { data, error } = await supabase
> 60	    .from('profiles')
> 61	    .select('mco_balance')
> 62	    .eq('id', userId)
> 63	    .maybeSingle();
> 64	
> 65	  if (error) throw new Error(error.message);
> 66	  return (data?.mco_balance as number | null) ?? 0;
> 67	}
> 68	
> 69	/**
> 70	 * Returns true if the user has enough mcoCoins for the operation.
> 71	 * Prefer this for UI guard checks (non-destructive read).
> 72	 */
> 73	export async function hasSufficientBalance(
> 74	  supabase: SupabaseClient,
> 75	  userId: string,
> 76	  required: number,
> 77	): Promise<boolean> {
> 78	  const balance = await getMcoBalance(supabase, userId);
> 79	  return balance >= required;
> 80	}
> 81	
> 82	/**
> 83	 * Convenience wrapper: checks balance then deducts in sequence.
> 84	 * Use this in Edge Functions where you want an explicit pre-check message.
> 85	 */
> 86	export async function checkAndDeductCoins(
> 87	  supabase: SupabaseClient,
> 88	  userId: string,
> 89	  operation: CoinOperation,
> 90	): Promise<void> {
> 91	  const cost = COIN_COSTS[operation];
> 92	  const sufficient = await hasSufficientBalance(supabase, userId, cost);
> 93	  if (!sufficient) {
> 94	    throw new Error(
> 95	      `Saldo insuficiente: operação "${operation}" custa ${cost} mcoCoins`,
> 96	    );
> 97	  }
> 98	  await deductMcoCoins(supabase, userId, cost);
> 99	}
> 100

> 7	 * OR direct PostgREST tampering). Also proves valid/invalid transitions, tenant guard (404), and idempotency.
> 8	 * Cost 0 (intent-execute governs; it never charges). Throwaway users + cascade cleanup.
> 9	 *
> 10	 * Usage: bun run scripts/qa/smoke-intent-execute.ts
> 11	 */
> 12	import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js';
> 13	
> 14	const URL = process.env.SUPABASE_URL ?? process.env.VITE_SUPABASE_URL!;
> 15	const SVC = (process.env.SB_SECRET_KEY ?? process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY)!;
> 16	const PUB = process.env.SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY ?? process.env.VITE_SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY ?? process.env.SUPABASE_ANON_KEY!;
> 17	if (!URL || !SVC || !PUB) { console.error('Missing env (URL/SVC/PUB)'); process.exit(1); }
> 18	
> 19	const admin = createClient(URL, SVC, { auth: { persistSession: false } });
> 20	const stamp = Date.now();
> 21	const uids: string[] = [];
> 22	let pass = true;
> 23	const check = (l: string, ok: boolean, d = '') => { console.log(`${ok ? '✅' : '❌'} ${l}${d ? `  → ${d}` : ''}`); if (!ok) pass = false; };
> 24	
> 25	async function mkUser(tag: string): Promise<{ uid: string; token: string }> {
> 26	  const email = `ie-${tag}-${stamp}@example.com`, password = `Aa1!${crypto.randomUUID()}`;
> 27	  const { data: cu, error } = await admin.auth.admin.createUser({ email, password, email_confirm: true });
> 28	  if (error || !cu?.user) throw new Error(`createUser(${tag}): ${error?.message}`);
> 29	  uids.push(cu.user.id);
> 30	  const userClient = createClient(URL, PUB, { auth: { persistSession: false } });
> 31	  const { data: si, error: siErr } = await userClient.auth.signInWithPassword({ email, password });
> 32	  if (siErr || !si?.session) throw new Error(`signIn(${tag}): ${siErr?.message}`);
> 33	  return { uid: cu.user.id, token: si.session.access_token };
> 34	}
> 35	
> 36	async function insertPlan(uid: string, plan: unknown, status: string, policy: string): Promise<string> {
> 37	  const { data, error } = await admin.from('intent_plans')
> 38	    .insert({ user_id: uid, intent: 'smoke intent', plan, status, policy, rationale: 'smoke' })
> 39	    .select('id').single();
> 40	  if (error || !data) throw new Error(`insertPlan: ${error?.message}`);
> 41	  return data.id as string;
> 42	}
> 43	
> 44	async function exec(token: string | null, body: unknown) {
> 45	  const headers: Record<string, string> = { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', apikey: PUB };
> 46	  if (token) headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${token}`;
> 47	  const r = await fetch(`${URL}/functions/v1/intent-execute`, { method: 'POST', headers, body: JSON.stringify(body) });
> 48	  let j: { plan_status?: string; code?: string; already?: boolean; error?: string } = {};
> 49	  const t = await r.text(); try { j = JSON.parse(t); } catch { /* keep {} */ }
> 50	  return { status: r.status, body: j };
> 51	}
> 52	const dbStatus = async (id: string) => (await admin.from('intent_plans').select('status').eq('id', id).single()).data?.status;
> 53	
> 54	const LOW = [{ action: 'revisar intenção', channel: 'none', impact: 'low' }];
> 55	const HIGH = [{ action: 'enviar email de boas-vindas', channel: 'email', impact: 'high' }];
> 56	const HIGH_SOCIAL = [{ action: 'publicar post no LinkedIn', channel: 'social', impact: 'high' }];
> 57	
> 58	try {
> 59	  const u1 = await mkUser('owner');
> 60	  const u2 = await mkUser('intruder');
> 61	
> 62	  // Seed plans for u1.
> 63	  const A = await insertPlan(u1.uid, LOW, 'auto_approved', 'auto');         // legit low-impact auto
> 64	  const B = await insertPlan(u1.uid, HIGH, 'pending_hitl', 'hitl');         // legit high-impact awaiting approval
> 65	  const C = await insertPlan(u1.uid, HIGH_SOCIAL, 'auto_approved', 'auto'); // TAMPERED: high-impact stored as auto_approved
> 66	  const D = await insertPlan(u1.uid, HIGH, 'pending_hitl', 'hitl');         // for reject path
> 67	  // E: TAMPERED plan SHAPE — non-array jsonb (object) carrying a high-impact task, stored auto_approved.
> 68	  const E = await insertPlan(u1.uid, { task: { action: 'publicar post', channel: 'social', impact: 'high' } }, 'auto_approved', 'auto');
> 69	
> 70	  // ── 1. Input/auth guards ──
> 71	  check('no-auth → 401', (await exec(null, { plan_id: A, decision: 'execute' })).status === 401);
> 72	  check('missing plan_id → 422', (await exec(u1.token, { decision: 'execute' })).status === 422);
> 73	  check('bad decision → 422', (await exec(u1.token, { plan_id: A, decision: 'nuke' })).status === 422);
> 74	
> 75	  // ── 2. Tenant guard: intruder cannot touch u1's plan → 404 ──
> 76	  check('cross-tenant execute → 404 (tenant guard)', (await exec(u2.token, { plan_id: A, decision: 'execute' })).status === 404);
> 77	
> 78	  // ── 3. Low-impact auto plan executes without approval ──
> 79	  const eA = await exec(u1.token, { plan_id: A, decision: 'execute' });
> 80	  check('A low-impact auto → 200 executed', eA.status === 200 && eA.body.plan_status === 'executed', `status=${eA.status} ps=${eA.body.plan_status}`);
> 81	  check('A persisted executed', (await dbStatus(A)) === 'executed');
> 82	
> 83	  // ── 4. THE GOLDEN RULE: tampered auto_approved HIGH-impact plan is REFUSED on execute ──
> 84	  const eC = await exec(u1.token, { plan_id: C, decision: 'execute' });
> 85	  check('C tampered auto_approved high-impact → 409 hitl_required (re-validation)', eC.status === 409 && eC.body.code === 'hitl_required', `status=${eC.status} code=${eC.body.code}`);
> 86	  check('C NOT executed (still auto_approved)', (await dbStatus(C)) === 'auto_approved', `status=${await dbStatus(C)}`);
> 87	  // and it cannot be silently approved either (approve only from pending_hitl)
> 88	  check('C approve on auto_approved → 409 (invalid transition)', (await exec(u1.token, { plan_id: C, decision: 'approve' })).status === 409);
> 89	
> 90	  // ── 4b. FAIL-CLOSED: a non-array (malformed/tampered) plan is treated as high-impact → blocked ──
> 91	  const eE = await exec(u1.token, { plan_id: E, decision: 'execute' });
> 92	  check('E malformed non-array plan → 409 hitl_required (fail-closed)', eE.status === 409 && eE.body.code === 'hitl_required', `status=${eE.status} code=${eE.body.code}`);
> 93	  check('E NOT executed', (await dbStatus(E)) === 'auto_approved', `status=${await dbStatus(E)}`);
> 94	
> 95	  // ── 5. Legit HITL path: pending_hitl → approve → execute ──
> 96	  const exBefore = await exec(u1.token, { plan_id: B, decision: 'execute' });
> 97	  check('B execute before approval → 409 hitl_required', exBefore.status === 409 && exBefore.body.code === 'hitl_required', `status=${exBefore.status} code=${exBefore.body.code}`);
> 98	  const apB = await exec(u1.token, { plan_id: B, decision: 'approve' });
> 99	  check('B approve → 200 approved', apB.status === 200 && apB.body.plan_status === 'approved', `ps=${apB.body.plan_status}`);
> 100	  const exB = await exec(u1.token, { plan_id: B, decision: 'execute' });
> 101	  check('B execute after approval → 200 executed', exB.status === 200 && exB.body.plan_status === 'executed', `ps=${exB.body.plan_status}`);
> 102	  check('B idempotent execute → already=true', (await exec(u1.token, { plan_id: B, decision: 'execute' })).body.already === true);
> 103	
> 104	  // ── 6. Reject path is terminal ──
> 105	  const rjD = await exec(u1.token, { plan_id: D, decision: 'reject' });
> 106	  check('D reject → 200 rejected', rjD.status === 200 && rjD.body.plan_status === 'rejected', `ps=${rjD.body.plan_status}`);
> 107	  check('D execute after reject → 409', (await exec(u1.token, { plan_id: D, decision: 'execute' })).status === 409);
> 108	} catch (e) {
> 109	  pass = false; console.error('❌ smoke error:', e instanceof Error ? e.message : e);
> 110	} finally {
> 111	  for (const id of uids) await admin.auth.admin.deleteUser(id);
> 112	  console.log(`\n🧹 cleanup: ${uids.length} throwaway user(s) deleted (intent_plans cascade)`);
> 113	  console.log(pass ? '\n✅✅ INTENT-EXECUTE HITL SMOKE PASSED' : '\n❌ INTENT-EXECUTE HITL SMOKE FAILED');
> 114	  process.exit(pass ? 0 : 1);
> 115	}
> 116	
> ```

## 🤖 Claude *(01:53:04)*

Complete material picture now. orchestrate-content takes `{topic, platforms, wp_status}` and returns fast with a `run_id` (async flywheel), 402 on no balance — perfect for a real dispatch driven from the plan's `intent`. The UI hook (`useIntentOrchestrator`) only SELECTs + calls edge fns (no direct `.update()` on `intent_plans`), so a transition-guard trigger won't break the UI.

Two quick verifications before writing the migration: composite-FK feasibility (tenant-safe bindings) + the exact `auth.role()` idiom already used in this codebase.

## 🤖 Claude *(01:55:54)*

All verifications green:
- `campaigns` has `uniq_campaigns_id_user UNIQUE(id,user_id)`; `sequence_enrollments.id` is PK. I'll use **plain FKs + `ON DELETE SET NULL`** (composite FK + SET NULL would try to null the `NOT NULL` `user_id` — wrong) plus a **write-time binding tenant-validation trigger** for defense-in-depth.
- `auth.role() IS DISTINCT FROM 'service_role'` is the exact proven idiom (migration `20260602150000`). Mirroring it.
- The intent unit tests mock `outward_dispatch: 'gated'` — I'll keep `'gated'` in the union so they stay valid.

Now building. **Law 2 first: SOP before code.** Writing the SOP update, the migration, and the rewritten executor in one batch (independent files).

```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/docs/processes/[[intent-plan-execution|intent-plan-execution]].md",
  "content": "# SOP \u2014 Intent Plan Execution (HITL approve / reject / execute + outward dispatch)\n\n> **Lei 2 (Processo Antecipado).** Documenta o processo humano da metade HITL do FR-MH-009 (Intent Orchestrator) **e** o disparo outward real (un-gate v6.27.1) **antes** do c\u00f3digo. SSOT: `docs/bok/marketing-hub/04-frd.md` FR-MH-009 + `05-sdd.md` (\u00a7intent-execute + STRIDE \"a\u00e7\u00e3o de alto impacto sem revis\u00e3o = Tampering \u2192 policy engine + HITL obrigat\u00f3rio\") + `06-data-model.md` (\u00a7intent_plans status machine + bindings + transition guard).\n\n## Contexto\n\n`intent-orchestrate` (FR-MH-009) traduz uma inten\u00e7\u00e3o em linguagem natural num **plano de tarefas** e roda um **policy engine**: qualquer tarefa de **alto impacto** (publicar / enviar / cobrar / veicular an\u00fancio / canal outward) for\u00e7a `policy='hitl'` + `status='pending_hitl'`; sen\u00e3o `policy='auto'` + `status='auto_approved'`. O plano fica persistido em `intent_plans` com um `rationale` (XAI audit\u00e1vel). Este SOP cobre o que acontece **depois**: aprovar, rejeitar, executar \u2014 e, na execu\u00e7\u00e3o, **disparar a a\u00e7\u00e3o outward real**.\n\nM\u00e1quina de estados (`intent_plans.status`):\n\n```\nproposed \u2500\u2500\u25b6 auto_approved \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u25b6 executed \u2500\u2500\u25b6 [outward dispatch]\n        \u2514\u2500\u2500\u25b6 pending_hitl \u2500\u2500\u25b6 approved \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u25b6 executed \u2500\u2500\u25b6 [outward dispatch]\n                          \u2514\u2500\u2500\u25b6 rejected  (terminal)\n```\n\n## Operator\n\nQuem executa hoje: o **tenant** (Usu\u00e1rio Zero / operador de marketing) revisando os planos no **Marketing Hub** (`/dashboard/marketing`, painel Intent Orchestrator). A decis\u00e3o HITL \u00e9 **humana e consciente** \u2014 \u00e9 a salvaguarda contra uma a\u00e7\u00e3o outward disparar sem revis\u00e3o.\n\n## Sequence (cada passo com crit\u00e9rio material de sucesso)\n\n| # | Passo | A\u00e7\u00e3o | Crit\u00e9rio de sucesso material |\n|---|-------|------|------------------------------|\n| 1 | **Orquestrar** | Operator descreve a inten\u00e7\u00e3o \u2192 `intent-orchestrate` | HTTP 200 `{plan_id, policy, plan_status, tasks, rationale}` + linha em `intent_plans` |\n| 2 | **Triagem auto** | Se `policy='auto'` (s\u00f3 tarefas low/medium, nenhum canal outward) | `status='auto_approved'` \u2014 n\u00e3o exige humano |\n| 3 | **Triagem HITL** | Se `policy='hitl'` (\u22651 tarefa de alto impacto) | `status='pending_hitl'` \u2014 aparece no painel HITL pendente |\n| 4 | **Decidir** | Operator clica **Aprovar** ou **Rejeitar** \u2192 `intent-execute {decision}` | `status='approved'` ou `'rejected'` (transi\u00e7\u00e3o validada server-side) |\n| 5 | **Executar** | Operator clica **Executar** \u2192 `intent-execute {decision:'execute'}` | `intent-execute` **re-valida** o outward; se passa, `status='executed'` **e dispara o canal real** (manifesto `outward_dispatch` + `dispatch[]`) |\n\n## Roteamento do dispatch outward (un-gate v6.27.1)\n\nAp\u00f3s a governan\u00e7a (`status='executed'`), o executor dispara **uma** a\u00e7\u00e3o outward real, por preced\u00eancia das *bindings* do plano, encaminhando o **JWT do chamador** (a cascata fatura o **mesmo tenant** sob RLS):\n\n| Preced\u00eancia | Condi\u00e7\u00e3o | Alvo | Custo (self-bill do alvo) |\n|-------------|----------|------|----------------------------|\n| 1 | `intent_plans.campaign_id` setado | `campaign-run {campaign_id}` | 10 (fee) + 10\u00d7N passos |\n| 2 | `intent_plans.enrollment_id` setado | `nurture-dispatch {enrollment_id}` | 2 (consent-gated) |\n| 3 | sen\u00e3o, \u22651 canal `content`/`affiliate`/`social` nas tarefas | `orchestrate-content {topic: intent, platforms}` | 10 |\n| \u2014 | nenhum canal outward (s\u00f3 `none`/`email` sem enrollment) | \u2014 | `outward_dispatch='none'` (s\u00f3 governan\u00e7a) |\n\nMapa canal\u2192plataforma (espelha `campaign-run`): `content`\u2192`[wordpress]` \u00b7 `affiliate`\u2192`[wordpress]` \u00b7 `social`\u2192`[linkedin,twitter]` \u00b7 `email`/`none`/desconhecido \u2192 territ\u00f3rio de `nurture-dispatch` (exige `enrollment_id`), **n\u00e3o** serv\u00edvel via `orchestrate-content`.\n\n**Kill-switch (self-protection / self-healing):** `INTENT_OUTWARD_DISPATCH_ENABLED=false` (secret) reverte o executor para **governan\u00e7a-only** (`outward_dispatch='gated'`) sem redeploy \u2014 uso em resposta a incidente. Default = `true` (ativo/un-gated).\n\n## Verification gates (defesa-em-profundidade \u2014 a regra de ouro)\n\n1. **Transi\u00e7\u00f5es v\u00e1lidas server-side.** `intent-execute` recusa transi\u00e7\u00f5es inv\u00e1lidas (`approve` s\u00f3 de `pending_hitl`; `execute` nunca de `rejected`; `executed` \u00e9 idempotente \u2014 **n\u00e3o re-dispara**). Retorna `409` com mensagem PT-BR.\n2. **Re-valida\u00e7\u00e3o no execute (N\u00c3O confiar em `auto_approved`).** O `execute` **recomputa** o high-impact a partir do `plan` armazenado (mesmo gate do `intent-orchestrate`, via `_shared/intent-policy.ts`). Se o plano cont\u00e9m a\u00e7\u00e3o de alto impacto **e** `status !== 'approved'`, recusa com `409 {code:'hitl_required'}` \u2014 mesmo que a linha diga `policy='auto'`/`status='auto_approved'` (prote\u00e7\u00e3o contra mislabel da IA **ou** adultera\u00e7\u00e3o direta do registro via PostgREST RLS). FAIL-CLOSED: plano n\u00e3o-array (jsonb adulterado) \u00e9 tratado como alto impacto.\n3. **Tenant guard.** `intent-execute` carrega o plano por `id` **E** `user_id = auth.uid()` (service role + escopo expl\u00edcito). Plano de outro tenant \u2192 `404`.\n4. **OTD-INTENT-TRANSITION-GUARD (DB, defesa-em-profundidade).** Trigger `tr_guard_intent_plan_transition` (BEFORE UPDATE, `SECURITY DEFINER`, `search_path=''`) permite que **s\u00f3 o `service_role`** mova `status` para `approved` ou `executed`. Um tenant que tente `PATCH intent_plans?id=eq.X {status:'approved'}` direto via PostgREST (RLS `update_own`) \u00e9 **bloqueado** (`RAISE EXCEPTION 42501`) \u2014 n\u00e3o consegue se auto-aprovar e cavalgar a re-valida\u00e7\u00e3o at\u00e9 um dispatch real. Tentativa n\u00e3o-autorizada \u00e9 logada via `RAISE WARNING` no **log do Postgres** (sobrevive ao rollback que o EXCEPTION causa; um INSERT em `infra_health_logs` aqui faria rollback junto com a statement abortada \u2014 por isso o forense vai pro server log, n\u00e3o pra tabela).\n5. **Binding tenant-validation (DB).** Trigger `tr_validate_intent_plan_bindings` (BEFORE INSERT/UPDATE) exige que `campaign_id`/`enrollment_id` setados perten\u00e7am ao `user_id` do plano (`RAISE EXCEPTION 42501`) \u2014 em cima do guard de posse j\u00e1 feito pelos pr\u00f3prios `campaign-run`/`nurture-dispatch` no momento do dispatch (entidade alheia \u2192 `404`, dispatch inerte).\n6. **Outward dispatch \u00e9 real, mas billing/consent ficam com o alvo.** O executor **n\u00e3o** cobra (custo 0); cada alvo self-bila e aplica seus pr\u00f3prios gates (`orchestrate-content`/`campaign-run` \u2192 `402` sem saldo; `nurture-dispatch` \u2192 consent gate). Um `402`/`404`/`blocked` vindo do alvo \u00e9 prova material de que o dispatch **\u00e9 real** (c\u00f3digo gated nunca produziria erro do alvo) e aparece em `dispatch[].http_status`.\n\n## Recovery path (falha no passo N)\n\n- **Transi\u00e7\u00e3o inv\u00e1lida (409):** o estado n\u00e3o muda; o Operator corrige (ex.: aprovar antes de executar). Nada a fazer no banco.\n- **Re-valida\u00e7\u00e3o bloqueia (409 `hitl_required`):** o plano permanece `pending_hitl`/`auto_approved`; o Operator deve **Aprovar** explicitamente antes de re-executar. Esse bloqueio \u00e9 o comportamento correto, n\u00e3o um bug.\n- **Transition guard bloqueia (42501):** um tenant tentou mover status para approved/executed direto no banco. Comportamento correto (ataque/erro). O estado n\u00e3o muda; revisar o log do Postgres (`intent_plans transition guard: blocked ...`).\n- **Dispatch falha p\u00f3s-execu\u00e7\u00e3o (`outward_dispatch='failed'`):** o plano J\u00c1 est\u00e1 `executed` (governan\u00e7a feita \u2014 idempot\u00eancia protege contra dobro de cobran\u00e7a no retry), mas o alvo retornou non-2xx (ex.: `402` sem saldo, `404` binding alheio). O `dispatch[].detail` traz o motivo. O Operator resolve a causa (saldo / binding / consent) e **re-orquestra** um novo plano para disparar de novo \u2014 re-executar o mesmo plano retorna `already=true` (n\u00e3o re-dispara). **OTD-INTENT-DISPATCH-REFUND** (aberta): avaliar refund autom\u00e1tico quando o alvo falha p\u00f3s-transi\u00e7\u00e3o (espelha OTD-AEO-REFUND).\n- **Falha de persist\u00eancia (500):** `infra_health_logs.service='intent-execute' status='unhealthy'`; o status n\u00e3o avan\u00e7a; retry idempotente seguro (a transi\u00e7\u00e3o \u00e9 checada de novo).\n\n## Success signal (materialmente observ\u00e1vel)\n\n`intent_plans.status='executed'` para o `plan_id` + `outward_dispatch \u2208 {dispatched, none}` (com `dispatch[]` mostrando o alvo + `http_status 2xx` quando houve envio) + um n\u00f3 `observation` no Knowledge Mesh (`intent-exec-<plan_id>`) + `infra_health_logs.service='intent-execute' status='healthy'`. Um plano de alto impacto **s\u00f3** chega a `executed` se passou por `approved` (humano) \u2014 verific\u00e1vel no `created_at`/`updated_at` e no hist\u00f3rico de status, e agora **garantido no n\u00edvel do banco** pela transition guard.\n\n## ORO\n\n- **Operator:** tenant (humano) no painel HITL \u00b7 **Reviewer:** o policy engine + re-valida\u00e7\u00e3o server-side + transition guard (DB) + `/security-review` da migration \u00b7 **Owner:** Sovereign (blast radius = a\u00e7\u00e3o outward + gasto real de mcoCoins disparados p\u00f3s-aprova\u00e7\u00e3o; mitigado pela re-valida\u00e7\u00e3o fail-closed, pela transition guard service-role-only, pelo gate HITL humano e pelos billing/consent gates dos alvos).\n"
}

  "file_path": "/home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/supabase/migrations/20260603270000_intent_plans_transition_guard_and_bindings.sql",
  "content": "-- Migration: intent_plans OTD-INTENT-TRANSITION-GUARD + outward dispatch bindings (FR-MH-009 un-gate, v6.27.1).\n-- BoK SSOT: docs/bok/marketing-hub/06-data-model.md (\u00a7intent_plans) + 04-frd.md FR-MH-009 + 05-sdd.md\n--           (\u00a7intent-execute outward dispatch + STRIDE \"a\u00e7\u00e3o de alto impacto sem revis\u00e3o = Tampering \u2192 HITL\").\n-- SOP: docs/processes/[[intent-plan-execution|intent-plan-execution]].md.\n--\n-- TWO defenses-in-depth that arm the executor to dispatch outward safely:\n--   (1) OTD-INTENT-TRANSITION-GUARD \u2014 a BEFORE UPDATE trigger that lets ONLY the service_role move status to\n--       'approved' or 'executed'. The HITL approval AND the execute transition both run through intent-execute\n--       (service role). A tenant tampering with their OWN row via PostgREST (RLS update_own grants UPDATE on own\n--       rows, incl. the status column) is blocked here \u2014 so they cannot self-approve past the human HITL gate and\n--       then ride intent-execute's re-validation (which trusts status='approved') into a real outward dispatch.\n--   (2) Binding tenant-validation \u2014 campaign_id / enrollment_id let the executor route to campaign-run /\n--       nurture-dispatch; a BEFORE INSERT/UPDATE trigger enforces the bound entity belongs to the plan's owner\n--       (defense-in-depth ON TOP of the dispatch-time ownership guards already in those edge fns: a foreign id\n--       there yields 404, an inert dispatch \u2014 this stops the junk binding from ever being stored).\n\n-- \u2500\u2500 Outward dispatch bindings (nullable; the executor routes on them). Plain FK + ON DELETE SET NULL:\n--    a composite (id,user_id) FK with SET NULL would try to NULL the NOT NULL user_id on parent delete \u2014 wrong;\n--    the tenant match is enforced by tr_validate_intent_plan_bindings below instead. \u2500\u2500\nALTER TABLE public.intent_plans\n  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS campaign_id   uuid REFERENCES public.campaigns(id)            ON DELETE SET NULL,\n  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS enrollment_id uuid REFERENCES public.sequence_enrollments(id) ON DELETE SET NULL;\n\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_intent_plans_campaign   ON public.intent_plans (campaign_id)   WHERE campaign_id   IS NOT NULL;\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_intent_plans_enrollment ON public.intent_plans (enrollment_id) WHERE enrollment_id IS NOT NULL;\n\n-- \u2500\u2500 (1) Transition guard: only service_role may set status \u2192 approved | executed \u2500\u2500\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.guard_intent_plan_transition()\nRETURNS trigger\nLANGUAGE plpgsql\nSECURITY DEFINER\nSET search_path = ''\nAS $$\nBEGIN\n  IF NEW.status IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.status\n     AND NEW.status IN ('approved', 'executed')\n     AND auth.role() IS DISTINCT FROM 'service_role'\n  THEN\n    -- Self-protection: a forensic line to the Postgres server log. It survives the rollback that the EXCEPTION\n    -- triggers \u2014 an INSERT into infra_health_logs here would be rolled back with the aborted statement, so it\n    -- could not be persisted to a table. Errors (incl. this EXCEPTION) are logged by Postgres regardless.\n    RAISE WARNING 'intent_plans transition guard: blocked %->% on plan % (role=%)',\n      OLD.status, NEW.status, OLD.id, COALESCE(auth.role(), 'none');\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'Transi\u00e7\u00e3o para \"%\" exige service_role (use o fluxo HITL via intent-execute).', NEW.status\n      USING ERRCODE = '42501';\n  END IF;\n  RETURN NEW;\nEND;\n$$;\n\nREVOKE EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.guard_intent_plan_transition() FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;\n\nDROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS tr_guard_intent_plan_transition ON public.intent_plans;\nCREATE TRIGGER tr_guard_intent_plan_transition\n  BEFORE UPDATE ON public.intent_plans\n  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION public.guard_intent_plan_transition();\n\n-- \u2500\u2500 (2) Binding tenant-validation: a bound campaign/enrollment must belong to the plan's owner \u2500\u2500\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.validate_intent_plan_bindings()\nRETURNS trigger\nLANGUAGE plpgsql\nSECURITY DEFINER\nSET search_path = ''\nAS $$\nBEGIN\n  IF NEW.campaign_id IS NOT NULL\n     AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.campaigns c WHERE c.id = NEW.campaign_id AND c.user_id = NEW.user_id)\n  THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'campaign_id % n\u00e3o pertence ao tenant do plano.', NEW.campaign_id USING ERRCODE = '42501';\n  END IF;\n  IF NEW.enrollment_id IS NOT NULL\n     AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.sequence_enrollments e WHERE e.id = NEW.enrollment_id AND e.user_id = NEW.user_id)\n  THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'enrollment_id % n\u00e3o pertence ao tenant do plano.', NEW.enrollment_id USING ERRCODE = '42501';\n  END IF;\n  RETURN NEW;\nEND;\n$$;\n\nREVOKE EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.validate_intent_plan_bindings() FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;\n\nDROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS tr_validate_intent_plan_bindings ON public.intent_plans;\nCREATE TRIGGER tr_validate_intent_plan_bindings\n  BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON public.intent_plans\n  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION public.validate_intent_plan_bindings();\n"
}

A plan containing a high-impact task can\n// only execute if a human has explicitly approved it (status='approved'), even if the row says auto_approved (AI\n// mislabel OR direct PostgREST tampering). Defense-in-depth at the DB: tr_guard_intent_plan_transition lets ONLY\n// the service_role move status to approved/executed (migration 20260603270000), so a tenant cannot self-approve\n// via PostgREST and ride this re-validation into a dispatch.\n//\n// OUTWARD DISPATCH (un-gate v6.27.1): after governance (status=executed) the executor performs the REAL outward\n// action, routing on the plan's bindings (campaign_id \u2192 campaign-run \u00b7 enrollment_id \u2192 nurture-dispatch \u00b7 else\n// content/social/affiliate channels \u2192 orchestrate-content), forwarding the caller's JWT so the whole cascade\n// self-bills the SAME tenant under RLS. Cost 0 here: it governs; the target self-bills + applies its own gates.\n// Kill-switch INTENT_OUTWARD_DISPATCH_ENABLED=false reverts to governance-only (self-protection). JWT-guarded\n// (verify_jwt=false + in-function getUser \u2014 edge-jwt SOP).\nimport { serve } from \"https://deno.land/std@0.168.0/http/server.ts\";\nimport { createClient } from \"https://esm.sh/@supabase/supabase-js@2\";\nimport { planIsHighImpact, type PlanTask } from \"../_shared/intent-policy.ts\";\n\nconst corsHeaders = {\n  \"Access-Control-Allow-Origin\": \"*\",\n  \"Access-Control-Allow-Methods\": \"POST, OPTIONS\",\n  \"Access-Control-Allow-Headers\": \"authorization, x-client-info, apikey, content-type\",\n};\n\ntype Decision = \"approve\" | \"reject\" | \"execute\";\nconst DECISIONS: Decision[] = [\"approve\", \"reject\", \"execute\"];\n\ninterface DispatchResult { target: string; http_status: number; ok: boolean; detail: string }\n\n// channel -> orchestrate-content platforms (mirrors campaign-run). email/none/unknown are nurture territory (need an\n// enrollment binding), NOT servable via orchestrate-content.\nfunction channelToPlatforms(channel?: string): string[] {\n  switch ((channel ?? \"\").toLowerCase()) {\n    case \"content\": return [\"wordpress\"];\n    case \"affiliate\": return [\"wordpress\"];\n    case \"social\": return [\"linkedin\", \"twitter\"];\n    default: return [];\n  }\n}\n\nserve(async (req) => {\n  if (req.method === \"OPTIONS\") return new Response(\"ok\", { headers: corsHeaders });\n  const json = (b: unknown, s = 200) =>\n    new Response(JSON.stringify(b), { status: s, headers: { ...corsHeaders, \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\" } });\n\n  const url = Deno.env.get(\"SUPABASE_URL\")!;\n  const serviceKey = (Deno.env.get(\"SB_SECRET_KEY\") ?? Deno.env.get(\"SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY\"))!;\n  const anonKey = (Deno.env.get(\"SB_PUBLISHABLE_KEY\") ?? Deno.env.get(\"SUPABASE_ANON_KEY\"))!;\n  const admin = createClient(url, serviceKey);\n  const logErr = () => admin.from(\"infra_health_logs\").insert({ service: \"intent-execute\", status: \"unhealthy\" }).then(() => {}, () => {});\n\n  try {\n    const authHeader = req.headers.get(\"Authorization\");\n    if (!authHeader) return json({ error: \"Missing authorization\" }, 401);\n    const userClient = createClient(url, anonKey, { global: { headers: { Authorization: authHeader } } });\n    const { data: { user }, error: authError } = await userClient.auth.getUser();\n    if (authError || !user) return json({ error: \"Unauthorized\" }, 401);\n\n    const { plan_id, decision } = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));\n    if (!plan_id || typeof plan_id !== \"string\") return json({ error: \"plan_id \u00e9 obrigat\u00f3rio\" }, 422);\n    if (!DECISIONS.includes(decision)) return json({ error: \"decision deve ser approve, reject ou execute\" }, 422);\n\n    // Tenant guard: load the plan scoped to the caller (service role + explicit user_id). Foreign plan \u2192 404.\n    const { data: row, error: loadErr } = await admin\n      .from(\"intent_plans\").select(\"*\").eq(\"id\", plan_id).eq(\"user_id\", user.id).maybeSingle();\n    if (loadErr) { logErr(); return json({ error: \"Falha ao carregar o plano\", detail: loadErr.message }, 500); }\n    if (!row) return json({ error: \"Plano n\u00e3o encontrado\" }, 404);\n\n    const status: string = row.status;\n\n    // \u2500\u2500 APPROVE: only a pending_hitl plan can be human-approved. \u2500\u2500\n    if (decision === \"approve\") {\n      if (status !== \"pending_hitl\") return json({ error: `N\u00e3o \u00e9 poss\u00edvel aprovar um plano em status \"${status}\"`, status }, 409);\n      const { error } = await admin.from(\"intent_plans\").update({ status: \"approved\" }).eq(\"id\", plan_id).eq(\"user_id\", user.id);\n      if (error) { logErr(); return json({ error: \"Falha ao aprovar\", detail: error.message }, 500); }\n      admin.from(\"infra_health_logs\").insert({ service: \"intent-execute\", status: \"healthy\" }).then(() => {}, () => {});\n      return json({ status: \"ok\", plan_id, plan_status: \"approved\" });\n    }\n\n    // \u2500\u2500 REJECT: terminal; allowed from any non-executed state (idempotent if already rejected). \u2500\u2500\n    if (decision === \"reject\") {\n      if (status === \"executed\") return json({ error: \"Plano j\u00e1 executado n\u00e3o pode ser rejeitado\", status }, 409);\n      if (status === \"rejected\") return json({ status: \"ok\", plan_id, plan_status: \"rejected\", already: true });\n      const { error } = await admin.from(\"intent_plans\").update({ status: \"rejected\" }).eq(\"id\", plan_id).eq(\"user_id\", user.id);\n      if (error) { logErr(); return json({ error: \"Falha ao rejeitar\", detail: error.message }, 500); }\n      admin.from(\"infra_health_logs\").insert({ service: \"intent-execute\", status: \"healthy\" }).then(() => {}, () => {});\n      return json({ status: \"ok\", plan_id, plan_status: \"rejected\" });\n    }\n\n    // \u2500\u2500 EXECUTE: the security-critical path. \u2500\u2500\n    if (status === \"executed\") return json({ status: \"ok\", plan_id, plan_status: \"executed\", already: true }); // idempotent: no re-dispatch\n    if (status === \"rejected\") return json({ error: \"Plano rejeitado n\u00e3o pode ser executado\", status }, 409);\n\n    // DEFENSE-IN-DEPTH: re-run the SAME policy gate on the STORED plan. Never trust the persisted policy/status.\n    // A high-impact plan executes ONLY if a human explicitly approved it (status='approved').\n    const highImpact = planIsHighImpact(row.plan);\n    if (highImpact && status !== \"approved\") {\n      return json({\n        error: \"Plano de alto impacto exige aprova\u00e7\u00e3o humana (HITL) antes de executar.\",\n        code: \"hitl_required\", status,\n      }, 409);\n    }\n\n    // Mark executed FIRST (governance). Idempotency: a duplicate/concurrent execute now short-circuits on\n    // status='executed' above \u2192 no double dispatch, no double charge.\n    const { error: updErr } = await admin.from(\"intent_plans\")\n      .update({ status: \"executed\" }).eq(\"id\", plan_id).eq(\"user_id\", user.id);\n    if (updErr) { logErr(); return json({ error: \"Falha ao marcar como executado\", detail: updErr.message }, 500); }\n\n    // \u2500\u2500 OUTWARD DISPATCH (un-gate): route on bindings, forward the caller's JWT (cascade self-bills same tenant). \u2500\u2500\n    const dispatchEnabled = (Deno.env.get(\"INTENT_OUTWARD_DISPATCH_ENABLED\") ?? \"true\").toLowerCase() !== \"false\";\n    const tasks: PlanTask[] = Array.isArray(row.plan) ? row.plan : [];\n    const dispatches: DispatchResult[] = [];\n\n    const callFn = async (target: string, body: unknown): Promise<DispatchResult> => {\n      const ctrl = new AbortController();\n      const to = setTimeout(() => ctrl.abort(), 60_000);\n      try {\n        const resp = await fetch(`${url}/functions/v1/${target}`, {\n          method: \"POST\",\n          headers: { \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\", Authorization: authHeader },\n          body: JSON.stringify(body), signal: ctrl.signal,\n        });\n        let detail = \"\";\n        try { const jb = await resp.json(); detail = jb?.run_id ?? jb?.status ?? jb?.error ?? jb?.reason ?? \"\"; } catch { /* non-JSON */ }\n        return { target, http_status: resp.status, ok: resp.ok, detail: String(detail).slice(0, 200) };\n      } catch (e) {\n        const aborted = (e as Error)?.name === \"AbortError\";\n        return { target, http_status: 0, ok: false, detail: aborted ? \"timeout\" : String((e as Error)?.message ?? e).slice(0, 200) };\n      } finally { clearTimeout(to); }\n    };\n\n    let outward: \"gated\" | \"none\" | \"dispatched\" | \"failed\";\n    if (!dispatchEnabled) {\n      outward = \"gated\"; // kill-switch: governance-only.\n    } else if (row.campaign_id) {\n      dispatches.push(await callFn(\"campaign-run\", { campaign_id: row.campaign_id }));\n      outward = dispatches[dispatches.length - 1].ok ? \"dispatched\" : \"failed\";\n    } else if (row.enrollment_id) {\n      dispatches.push(await callFn(\"nurture-dispatch\", { enrollment_id: row.enrollment_id }));\n      outward = dispatches[dispatches.length - 1].ok ? \"dispatched\" : \"failed\";\n    } else {\n      const platforms = Array.from(new Set(tasks.flatMap((t) => channelToPlatforms(t.channel))));\n      if (platforms.length === 0) {\n        outward = \"none\"; // nothing outward to send (e.g., a low-impact 'none' plan) \u2014 governance only.\n      } else {\n        dispatches.push(await callFn(\"orchestrate-content\", { topic: String(row.intent).slice(0, 280), platforms, wp_status: \"draft\" }));\n        outward = dispatches[dispatches.length - 1].ok ? \"dispatched\" : \"failed\";\n      }\n    }\n\n    // Knowledge Mesh observation (mandate: every orchestration run). Fire-and-forget.\n    const taskCount = tasks.length;\n    admin.from(\"mcorch_nodes\").insert({\n      node_type: \"observation\", name: `intent-exec-${plan_id}`,\n      content: `Intent plan executed: \"${String(row.intent).slice(0, 140)}\" (${taskCount} tarefas \u00b7 high_impact=${highImpact}) \u00b7 outward=${outward}${dispatches.length ? ` [${dispatches.map((d) => `${d.target}:${d.http_status}`).join(\", \")}]` : \"\"}`,\n      user_id: user.id,\n    }).then(() => {}, () => {});\n    admin.from(\"infra_health_logs\").insert({ service: \"intent-execute\", status: outward === \"failed\" ? \"degraded\" : \"healthy\" }).then(() => {}, () => {});\n\n    return json({\n      status: \"ok\", plan_id, plan_status: \"executed\",\n      high_impact: highImpact, tasks: row.plan,\n      outward_dispatch: outward, dispatch: dispatches,\n    });\n  } catch (err) {\n    logErr();\n    return json({ error: \"Erro interno no intent-execute\", detail: String((err as Error)?.message ?? err) }, 500);\n  }\n});\n"
}
```
